**Last updated**: 2026-08-14 — **CURRENT STATE: prod = fly 232 (hotfix) · landing in LOCAL ITERATION MODE (D-B4.1-1, founder-ratified 2026-08-12).** **fly 232 = hero-spotlight horizontal-overflow hotfix**, readback-verified `revision` = **`2c1e5db772c692038fce3d6fca9dbd9f08f8d7b3`**. Defect: the page scrolled sideways with the hero text clipped left and blank space right — **founder-confirmed on a real phone**, live since B4. Cause: `.hero-spotlight` is 190% of the composer form (max-w-2xl), i.e. ~1277px centred on the viewport, so it overran the right edge on **every viewport under ~1277px** — phones, tablets and any non-maximised laptop, not just phones. Fix: `overflow-x-clip` on the first-viewport wrapper — **clip, never `hidden`** (hidden would create a scroll container and force the other axis to `auto`, and the ScrollTrigger pin is live in this build), and chosen over resizing the blob so desktop geometry is pixel-identical by construction. **B4.5 (local, 2026-08-14) — answer-pane auto-scroll fix (PRODUCT) + near-black panel + gradient removal + the deferred B4.4 hygiene.**
**(A) ANSWER PANE — the founder-reported defect is REAL, RESEARCH-ONLY, and was ONE line.** Investigation first: the entire repo contains **exactly one** answer-pane scroll site, `pages/research.tsx:258`, a `useEffect` on `[answer]` doing `scrollTop = scrollHeight` on **every token** — so at end-of-stream the user is parked at the BOTTOM of a long answer and must scroll UP to the Summary. **Verify and Explain do NOT share a component and do NOT duplicate it** — they are separate pages with **zero** scroll machinery (`git grep scrollTop|scrollIntoView|scrollTo` over `pages/`+`components/` returns only this site and the landing's chevron). Explain streams over SSE too but never auto-follows, so it cannot exhibit the defect; Verify does not even use `fetchEventSource`. **One fix, one surface, and nothing to carry across** — stated because Rule 19 would otherwise demand the sweep. **The completion signal is the `loading` true→false EDGE**, tracked with a `wasLoadingRef`: `loading` goes false in exactly two places, both genuine end-of-stream (`data.type === 'done'` at `research.tsx:438-439` and `onclose()` at :458) — **not a timeout, not the first gap in tokens.** ✅ **ORDERING CHECKED, NOT ASSUMED:** `loading` is *also* what swaps the raw stream for the composed render (`const sections = !loading ? parseResearchSections(...)`, plus the `ProvenanceLine`/`FallbackBanner` row, all gated on `!loading`), so both come from the SAME state change and React commits the composed DOM **before** the effect runs — the scroll lands on the finished Summary, not ahead of it. Scrolling to `0` is height-independent, so the re-render changing `scrollHeight` cannot invalidate it either. **DECISION ON FIGHTING THE USER — always snap, and the reason is that there is no user position to preserve:** the follow effect overwrites `scrollTop` on *every chunk*, so a manual scroll during generation is destroyed within one token; "the user deliberately scrolled away" is a state that **cannot survive to completion** in the current design. Given that, top-of-answer beats bottom-of-answer unconditionally. ⚠️ **Flagged, NOT changed:** making the follow YIELD to a manual scroll is a separate and larger change and was not in this baton — say so if it should be. Reduced motion gets `behavior:'auto'` (instant), everyone else `'smooth'`. **Regression test: `tests/test_research_answer_scroll.py`, 6 tests — and its scope is stated honestly in its own docstring.** They are SOURCE assertions, not behavioural: this repo has **no JS test runner**, and the completion path needs a live SSE stream, the FastAPI backend and a real credit-costing query to reach at all. What they do pin is the structure, including the one premise that can break **silently from a distance** — `test_composed_render_is_still_gated_on_loading`, because if the composed render is ever driven by a different trigger the ordering guarantee dissolves with nothing else complaining. Comments are stripped before matching (the fix is documented by a ~30-line comment naming every identifier asserted, so without stripping, deleting the whole implementation would leave the suite green). **Mutation-tested against the real file: 5/5 caught** (scroll deleted, edge guard replaced by the naive `if (loading) return`, reduced-motion ignored, stream-following removed, composed render un-gated). ⚠️ **END-TO-END NOT RUN** — a real query on `/research` is the remaining check and it is the founder's, since it needs the backend and spends credits.
**(B) NEAR-BLACK PANEL — founder-ratified, recorded as a KNOWING aesthetic decision, not a regression.** `--color-panel` **62 48 40 → 18 18 18**. This reverses B4.3's warm brown-charcoal deliberately: the founder has seen #3E3028 rendered and chose near-black anyway. What it trades away is kept in the CSS comment as the record — **the paper→panel lightness cliff returns to ~91 points (98.0% → 7.1%), WIDER than the 89 of the original ink panel**, and at **S=0%** the panel now sits **outside** the paper/hero hue family (H24°), which is the exact kinship B4.3 was built to create. **CONTRAST MEASURED IN-BROWSER from computed styles (not hand-estimated — and my hand computation agreed to within 0.01, checked precisely because the B4.1d session's estimate was wrong by 0.1):** headline/body paper@1.0 **17.95:1** (was 12.13) · sub paper@0.70 **9.08:1** (was 6.83) · CTA pill label **17.95:1** (was 12.13) · media hairline paper@0.15 **1.52:1** (decorative, not text). Every text value clears AA normal with far more margin than #3E3028 had, and with the gradient gone the panel's **weakest text is now the sub at 9.08:1**. **HAIRLINE REVERTED /20 → /15**: the B4.3 raise was compensation for the panel *lightening*, and that cause is gone — near-black is **darker than the ink the /15 hairline originally sat on** (1.52:1 now vs 1.55:1 historically = the original look restored; /20 would read 1.82:1). One-token flip if the founder disagrees.
**(C) GRADIENT REMOVED, headline solid + BOLD.** `.panel-gradient-text` is gone from the component (`spanClassName` dropped, `h2` gains `font-bold`; no color class on purpose — the panel wrapper's `text-paper` is inherited at full strength) and **entirely from `globals.css`**: the base rule, the per-line `background-size: 100% 1lh` tile, the `::selection` rule and the `forced-colors` fallback, replaced by a tombstone explaining that **all three existed only to make clipped text safe** and guard nothing once the glyphs have real ink. **The two B4.1d pytest guards are DELETED with their helpers** (`_GLOBALS`, `_CSS_COMMENT`, `_css`, `_gradient_block`) — not left in place, because `_gradient_block()` did `css.index()` on comment-stripped CSS and would have raised **ValueError**: a test that can only error is dead weight, not safety. **VERIFIED AT COMPUTED-STYLE LEVEL, which is stronger than any class check:** span `color` = paper, **`-webkit-text-fill-color` = paper (not transparent)**, **`background-image` = none**, class = `inline-block`. **The word reveal survives**: 24 spans, and forward/reverse readings are identical (below).
**(D) DEFERRED HYGIENE — all four done.** **D1** masters moved to **`assets-source/media/`** with a README carrying the exact re-derivation commands and the reason segment 1 ends at 31.00; `out/` drops **19.9 MB → 6.66 MB** and `out/media` now holds **exactly the two shipped files**. ### Task-A Answer-Quality QA + B2C/B2B decision (2026-06-15)

**Task-A medical-answer-quality QA is COMPLETE** (measurement only — NO product change, NO route decision made). Three layers run this session; all artifacts in `tests/results/` (gitignored):

- **Stage 1 — full 127-case golden eval** (`golden_results_20260613_144748.json`): raw 74.8% but heavily contaminated — 22/24 FAILs were harness/guard artifacts (v179 UI-language harness staleness ×6; guard over-block of colloquial symptom queries ×11; non-Latin-script identify ×4; transient ×1). Guard 100%, PHI 100%, Explain `no_fabricated_citations` hard-floor 0 violations, Explain disclaimer 17/17. **2 REAL content gaps**: R10 (digoxin toxicity omits nausea/vomiting + visual disturbances) + the persistent canonical-term under-coverage (R03 CHA₂DS₂-VASc, R16/R20 safer alternatives); R09 improved to PASS.
- **Stage 2 — citation-truth, 18 usable answers** (`citation_truth_20260614_113716.json`): PMID existence 80/80 (100%, **no fabricated PMIDs**); `has_hallucination` 0/20; mean source_support 90.6. Claim-support over 180 pairs: 45.6% supported / 50% partial / 3.9% unsupported / **0.6% contradicts (1)** / 0% wrong_scope. The 1 CONTRADICTS = polypharmacy/mortality reversal (answer "increased death risk" vs abstract OR 0.78 LOWER, PMID 35268461). B07 = confident AF-stroke-prevention answer with **0 citations**. **Lower-bound** (abstract-only judging over-counts unsupported).
- **Stage 3 — adversarial contradiction, 19 queries** (`adversarial_contradiction_20260614_232655.json`): famous-set reversal 3/16 = **18.8%** (C01 polypharmacy, C03 intensive-glucose, C15 DBP J-curve); cohort-specific 0/3 (underpowered, NOT evidence of safety); 4/186 pairs. C01 reproduced 3× ("reverts counterintuitive finding to intuition"). **Upper-bound** (loaded toward failure). So the true rate sits between ~0.6% (random) and ~19% (adversarial).

**Medical-advisor review:** REJECTED direct B2C public launch in the current state — the "reverses a counterintuitive finding *and* cites a real source" failure is clinically + legally unacceptable (TW 醫師法 §28 密醫罪, FDA device exposure). Recommended **3-phase roadmap**: **Phase 1 (≤1mo)** defensive pivot to B2B (pause B2C, reposition as clinician/pharmacist/researcher literature tool, HCP credential verification at signup, algorithm-oversight committee) → FDA-2026 non-device CDS exemption + avoids 密醫罪; **Phase 2 (3–6mo)** contradiction circuit-breaker + HealthBench-DDI ≥99% + HIPAA-2026 / ONC HTI-1; **Phase 3** SaMD registration (TFDA Class 2 + FDA 510(k)/De Novo) before gradual B2C.

**Prompt-fix attempt + re-measurement (2026-06-15, commit `96806b5`):** the direction-of-effect fidelity instruction (a 3-bullet block in `generator.py`) was applied and re-measured across 7 adversarial runs (2 baseline + 3 post-fix + the prior external baseline + a B01 Stage-2 recheck). **Verdict: PARTIAL mitigation — do NOT read it as "the rate dropped."** Post-fix adversarial famous-set reversal was **19% / 25% / 19%**, INSIDE the baseline band **19% / 25% / 38%** (overlapping; variance-dominated). What genuinely improved: the two stable, reproducible **NEUTRAL-FRAMED** reversers **C01** (polypharmacy/AF) and **C13** (smoker's-paradox STEMI) resolved to faithful (`same`) in all 3 post-fix runs — C01 stayed faithful even when retrieval cited a different PMID (35155632 vs 35268461), so the effect-statistic anchor is a general mechanism, not memorization (live probe showed "lower odds, OR 0.78"). **KEY NEW FINDING — FRAMING-SENSITIVITY:** on the loaded-premise Stage-2 query B01 ("*Why is polypharmacy a serious problem…*"), PMID 35268461 **STILL CONTRADICTS** under the fix — the premise overrides the instruction. Neutral framing fixed; loaded framing NOT — and real B2C users ask loaded questions. Also: C15 (conflicting-evidence + unstable retrieval) did not move; a mild `not_directional` drift (~5.3→~8.7) on non-targeted famous cases to watch; holdout held 0/5 in all 3 runs (no over-correction — but "no new harm" ≠ generalization to unseen reversal modes).

**Model-swap attempt + audit (2026-06-15, o4-mini via the §2.1 Provider abstraction; DONE — NEGATIVE):** swapping the Research generation model gpt-4.1 → o4-mini (reasoning model; needed the provider param-contract fix `455bcb5` to run) did **NOT** fix loaded-framing reversal. The initial o4-mini B01 read was counts-only ("3/3 no-contradict") and **did NOT reproduce**; a **persisted, full-prose** re-run (`tests/results/recheck_20260615_225242.json`, judge gpt-4.1) gave **0/3 fidelity**: run2 + run3 **CONTRADICTS** PMID 35268461 (asserting higher all-cause death "OR 1.82" while citing the OR-0.78-*lower* source — conflated the multimorbidity ORs onto polypharmacy); run1's "no-contradict" was an **evasion** (routed risk through multimorbidity, judge PARTIAL), not a faithful statement of OR 0.78. **No run stated the OR-0.78 lower-death finding.** **Framing-sensitivity is model-swap-resistant** — the SAME o4-mini, SAME PMID, on the NEUTRAL query (C01) stated *"counterintuitively linked to lower odds of all-cause death (OR 0.78)"* faithfully (even flagging the paradox) and attributed other cohorts' higher-mortality HRs to their sources; on the LOADED query (B01) it reversed 2/3. **Only framing differs.** **Scope caveat:** ONE loaded query (B01), 2/3 — the same small-sample limit as the prior optimistic read; not generalized. A GPT-5.4 comparison is an optional last check with LOW expected value (the failure looks systemic, not compute-limited).

**STRUCTURAL FINDING — the reversal problem is TWO distinct failure modes (2026-06-16; Phase-3 eval `evalrun_{o4-mini,gpt-4.1}_20260616` + N=10 retrieval-recall diagnostic `retrieval_recall_20260616_133331.json`, committed `4248dfd`):** this overturns the implicit "bad answers all come from generation" assumption. **(1) GENERATION-reversal (polypharmacy 35268461):** the protective OR-0.78 source is **reliably retrieved at loaded framing** (10/10 in pool AND final at L1/L2, rank 2, never reranked out) — yet the model still states the reversed direction. So the B01 reversals happened **with the source in hand** → a generation failure. A post-hoc direction (NLI) checker is the matching defense. **(2) RETRIEVAL-recall failure (obesity 37290898):** the protective paradox source is **often never retrieved** — loaded OB-L2 = **0/10 in pool** (recall-miss: the loaded rewrite → harm-mechanism/HFpEF papers, paradox doc never enters); neutral OB-L0 = in pool 7/10 but **reranked OUT 7/10** (enters low-scored ~rank 14, reranker keeps 5 newer higher-scored papers). The model **literally never sees** the paradox source and answers from harm-side papers → **a direction-checker CANNOT catch this** (no source in the answer's citations to compare against). Needs retrieval-side fixes. **Sub-findings:** neither anchor is in the local 690-doc store (100% FDA labels) — both are live-PubMed + LLM-rewrite, NOT local indexing. Framing biases retrieval itself, **oppositely by topic**: polypharmacy loaded recalls MORE (L2 10/10 vs L0 5/10), obesity loaded SUPPRESSES (L2 0/10 vs L0 7/10) — for obesity, framing and retrieval are the SAME root cause. **Hypothesis (unverified):** anchor title wording (polypharmacy paper = "Multimorbidity and Polypharmacy… Clinical Outcomes" matches loaded harm terms; obesity paper = neutral "Body mass index and survival" → loaded rewrite drifts off it). o4-mini vs gpt-4.1 on clean anchor-retrieved cases tied 3/6 each (same 3) — model-swap still doesn't fix generation-reversal. **Caveat:** single query-wording per topic, N=10, 2 topics — strong directional signal, not generalized; title-driver is a hypothesis.

**(i) RETRIEVAL prompt-fix attempt — FAILED + reverted (2026-06-16; `retrieval_recall_20260616_141620.json` vs `_133331.json`; retriever.py back at HEAD, NO commit):** the first retrieval-stabilization lever — a prompt-level **valence-neutral query-rewrite** instruction (strip the loaded premise from `_rewrite_query`) — failed both gates. **Step A (target):** OB-L2 anchor recall **0/10 → 0/10, no movement** — root cause is NOT valence but a **TERMINOLOGY mismatch**: the rewrite emits the lay term "obesity" while the anchor 37290898's title is "*Body mass index and survival*", so PubMed never matches it (neutralizing the premise can't fix obesity↔BMI). **Step B (regression guard) TRIPPED HARD:** **P-L1 collapsed 10/10 → 0/10** (deterministic, prompt-caused not variance — the neutralized rewrite shifted terms enough to lose a previously-reliable anchor), OB-L0 pool 7/10 → 0/10 — while P-L0 *improved* 5→10 in the SAME edit. **KEY LESSON:** a prompt-level "be neutral" instruction is **too BLUNT a lever** — anchor recall is highly sensitive to the precise search-term string, so a global rewrite instruction perturbs every query's terms unpredictably (fixes one, breaks another); there is **no single global rewrite instruction that simultaneously optimizes recall across topics** (P-L0 up while P-L1 down under the same edit proves it). **Caveat:** single query-wording per topic, N=10, 2 topics — directional, not a generalized "retrieval is unfixable" claim; the obesity↔BMI terminology cause is established for this anchor, the broader pattern is a hypothesis.

**③ DIRECTION-CHECKER prototype (the generation-side defense, BACKLOG §706a (b)) — 3 rounds (2026-06-16; cheap gpt-4.1-mini + factor-focused prompt; persisted answers `recheck_20260615_225242.json` + adversarial JSONs):** **the CORE (direction comparison) is validated; the WRAPPER (decomposition + aggregation) is where the risk lives.** **Round 1+2 (synthetic, single sentences):** detection 4/4 then 8/8 on known reversals, **0 false-positive on clean faithful**, conflation-trap 4/4 cross-topic (it correctly compares the claim's factor to *that factor's* direction even when the abstract reports both directions), terse abstracts 6/6 — so the cheap checker's direction logic generalizes. **Round 3 (REAL messy multi-claim answers, decompose→anchor→aggregate, polypharmacy):** detection **3/4 (one FALSE NEGATIVE)**, FP **1/2** — and every failure is in the WRAPPER, not the direction logic: **(1) decomposition-anchoring FN [the gating risk]** — B01-run2's real reversal slipped through because the decomposer extracted the claim with **empty cited_pmids** (nothing to compare); the *identical* claim in B01-run3 was caught because the decomposer happened to propagate the PMID — same reversal, opposite outcome, **false safety**; **(2) aggregation over-flag FP** — C01-115632 (faithful, correctly said OR 0.78 lower) flagged on a *different* sub-claim ("polypharmacy increases composite thrombotic events") that contradicts one cited source (32509316 null on stroke/SE) while agreeing with another (36495662) → the "ANY claim contradicts ANY source → flag" rule is too aggressive; **(3) over-splitting** (13–21 atomic claims/answer). **NEXT to evaluate — Path b-coarse:** a **WHOLE-ANSWER vs ANCHOR** direction comparison (compare the answer's overall directional stance on the key factor against the protective anchor's effect statistic directly, **bypassing per-claim decomposition/anchoring**) — hypothesis: catches the B01-run2 reversal the per-claim version missed. **Caveats:** one topic (polypharmacy), 7 real answers; the FP + VAGUE flag are debatable sub-claim tensions (so 1/2 FP is a ceiling — the protective-reversal itself was never falsely flagged); the tooling needed a mid-run fix (first attempt VOID — decomposition truncated at max_tokens=600). **Round 4 (Path b-coarse — whole-answer vs anchor, no decomposition):** fixes the gating FN — caught B01-run2 (the reversal per-claim missed) and all 4 real reversals (**detection 4/4**); its 1 FP is a genuinely-mixed answer (leads "higher HR 1.36-1.84", footnotes OR 0.78) so the flag is defensible; trade-off = coarse only sees the main-factor-vs-one-anchor reversal (blind to secondary-factor reversals) and needs an anchor-selection step. **Round 5 (anchor-selection validation — the last gating risk):** **naive selection ("the source the answer's main claim is based on") is CIRCULAR + whitewashing** — 0/3 on the confusion cases (answers citing BOTH protective 35268461-lower AND a higher source), it picks the higher source agreeing with the answer's reversed claim → whitewashes the reversal into a FALSE NEGATIVE (all wrong picks opposite-direction; this would gut the 4/4). The circularity DEFAULTS TO WHITEWASH (auto-finds the source that makes a reversed answer look fine). **ESCAPE (non-circular):** select by **COUNTERINTUITIVENESS-vs-clinical-prior** (which cited source reports the most surprising finding vs common medical assumption, judged by the source's OWN finding + the model's prior, INDEPENDENT of the answer's claim) — recovered **3/3** on the confusion cases; the prior is used only for selection, the direction judgment still comes from answer-vs-source → no circularity. **FOOTGUN:** requires **FULL abstracts** — with 700-char truncation it was 0/3 (the OR-0.78 sentence is buried deep in a stat-dense abstract that leads with the higher finding); full abstracts → 3/3. **VERDICT: coarse ③ is VIABLE but only under (counterintuitiveness-selection + full-abstract), NEVER naive selection.** **Open deps to pin before production:** (a) cross-topic prior reliability; (b) answers citing multiple competing counterintuitive sources (untested). **(a) DONE — VALIDATED 2026-06-17 (`anchor_prior_crosstab_20260617.json`, 6 topics across crisp/paradox/fuzzy bins, cheap gpt-4.1-mini):** counterintuitiveness-selection picked the correct anchor **5/5 on all confusion topics** (incl. 2 crisp paradoxes — obesity, ACCORD inverted-direction — and 2 fuzzy — HRT, alcohol); **0 mis-picks.** The bare prior was correct 5/6 (the one miss = HRT, a debatable GT-labeling artifact) — but **selection was MORE robust than the prior** (it reads the source's explicit effect size, so the one prior-miss still selected right). **A confidence gate is NOT the answer:** zero selection failures to gate, and confidence wouldn't discriminate anyway (the shaky HRT prior was high-confidence 0.8; the most-contested topic alcohol correctly drew the lowest conf 0.7). **Verdict: reliable as-is in this test; residual risk = a prior-miss that propagates to a selection error (not observed) — partly stressed by dep (b).** Caveats: 6 topics, synthetic answers (only polypharmacy real), one model.

**GENERATION-stage question-neutralization probe (2026-06-17, `neutralization_generation_20260617_105516.json`; polypharmacy/35268461 ONLY — proven retrieved, so retrieval is NOT a variable; RETRIEVE-ONCE-FREEZE-CONTEXT, generation-stage only, retrieval query untouched — NOT a repeat of the failed (i)):** decides the ③ division of labor. Retrieved once with the loaded B01 query, **35268461 confirmed in the frozen context**, then generated 4 phrasings × N=5 against the IDENTICAL frozen docs (current gpt-4.1 generator incl. fix `96806b5`), gpt-4.1 fidelity judge. **Result: loaded→neutral reversal 3/10 → 0/10 — neutralizing only the generator's question ELIMINATES the reversal** (neutral arm 10/10 faithful, both neutral phrasings agree → robust across 2 neutral phrasings on polypharmacy (single topic; cross-topic neutralization untested)). **In-run loaded control reproduced reversal (3/10)** → the fix did NOT fully solve it (control valid). **KEY NUANCE — loaded reversal is WORDING-SPECIFIC:** the two loaded phrasings DISAGREE — L1 (B01's vague "serious problem in elderly Asian patients") reverses 3/5, but L2 ("why does polypharmacy *worsen survival*") is **faithful 5/5** (the more-explicit premise self-corrected with OR 0.78). So "loaded→reversal" is not uniform — only the vague-harm-premise wording triggers it. **VERDICT (bin 1 — reversal ~eliminated under neutral): framing (the loaded premise) is the MAIN cause of the reversals that occur → front-end question-neutralization = a cheap effective pre-filter, ③ = a thin backstop; dep (b) pass-threshold = MEDIUM (the neutralization relaxation applies to the framing-induced-reversal path ONLY; dep (b) measures the source-multiplicity path, which front-end neutralization does NOT address — so the relaxation does NOT transfer). dep (b) subsequently PASSED at medium (4/4 correct-pick, see below).** Caveats: single topic, N=5/variant, one generator, 1 frozen context, synthetic neutralization phrasings; conditional on the anchor being retrieved (proven polypharmacy, unknown elsewhere); directional division-of-labor signal, not a production metric.

**dep (b) — competing counterintuitive sources, MEDIUM threshold — DONE/PASS (2026-06-17, `dep_b_competing_sources_20260617.json`):** the last untested selection stressor — when an answer cites TWO opposite-direction counterintuitive sources, does counterintuitiveness-selection still pick the right anchor (the source-multiplicity path, which front-end neutralization does NOT address). 4 two-source bundles (1 real polypharmacy reversal + 3 cross-topic synthetic, full abstracts): **(a) correct-pick 4/4, (b) whitewash 0, (c) fail-safe 0 → MEDIUM PASS** (the bar = pick right OR fail safe; achieved by picking right every time). It handled the two hardest: **glucose** (counterintuitive anchor is the *higher*-mortality ACCORD, INVERTED vs the others — still correct) and **HRT** (genuinely fuzzy-prior competing) — it reads each source's explicit effect size + prior, so a competing opposite-direction source did NOT confuse it. **Residual (carry):** the **fail-safe (abstain/low-conf) leg was NEVER exercised** (selection was accurate) → unproven; confidence was uniformly 0.9 even on contested HRT (the existing calibration debt persists → the fail-safe backstop is unreliable IF a future competing case stumps the selector). Caveats: synthetic answers except polypharmacy, 4 bundles, one model, single answer/bundle. **With dep (b) passing, selection ACCURACY is validated (dep a 5/5 + dep b 4/4). BUT the MEDIUM-threshold fail-safe leg (selector abstains/low-confidence when it cannot cleanly pick) was NEVER exercised — selection was accurate every time — and dep (a)+(b) both show the selector is OVERCONFIDENT on contested topics (uniform 0.9, wrong-but-0.8 on HRT). So the fail-safe safety net is UNVALIDATED and likely UNRELIABLE. DESIGN CONSTRAINT for production ③ (carry into the build spec): the flag/withhold trigger must NOT depend on the selector's self-reported confidence (it won't abstain — it will mis-pick confidently = the dangerous whitewash mode). Instead, trigger flag STRUCTURALLY — detect the *situation* 'answer cites ≥2 opposite-direction counterintuitive sources on the same factor→outcome' and flag unconditionally, regardless of selector confidence. Safety rests on situation-detection, not on the selector grading itself. What's left is PRODUCTION BUILD (engineering): the front-end vague-loaded-neutralization mechanism (unbuilt, §706a (b2) gap) + coarse ③ integration (unbuilt).**

**C1 (③) SHADOW BUILD — BUILT + VALIDATED → FAILED real-data shadow validation (2026-06-17, `direction_shadow_20260617_143022.json` + `…_rescore_nostruct_151126.json`; plan-back `C1_inrepo_planback.md`; code committed locally, NOT pushed, Fly deploy DEFERRED):** first prod-code touch in this chain. Gate 1 (credit probe through the real provider path) HEALTHY; Gate 2 push = no-op (`96806b5`/`fdd624c` already on origin/main); Fly deploy deferred (founder) — baseline captured in-process on HEAD (gpt-4.1, `96806b5` live). Built: `api/services/direction_checker.py` (async port of the validated core, 4 invariants), `server.py` shadow hook `_run_direction_check_background` behind `DIRECTION_CHECK_SHADOW` (default OFF, mirrors `_run_judge_background`, touches no SSE/answer), `scripts/direction_shadow_eval.py` (+ `_rescore`). Corpus frozen pre-generation (`tests/direction_shadow_corpus.json`: full 127 golden + 24 adversarial; live subset 43 = 25 targeted + 18 clean-faithful, clean criterion frozen first). **REAL NUMBERS — C1 as-built is NOT deployable (do NOT enable the flag): structural-ON detection 11/11 but FP 12/16 (75%), flags 37/43 (86% of ALL answers); structural-OFF (primary whole-answer-vs-anchor only) detection 0/11, FP 1/16.** The "11/11" is an ARTIFACT of the structural trigger rubber-stamping `two_opposite_counterintuitive=True` on real 4–5-source retrievals (35/43 verdict=structural, incl. unambiguous single-direction topics — peanut-LEAP, beta-blockers-HFrEF, digoxin, serotonin-syndrome) → no precision. The primary path detects NOTHING because **C1's own cheap anchor-scorer fell into the exact B01 conflation trap it exists to catch** (P-L1: read the multimorbidity OR 2.04 instead of polypharmacy OR 0.78 from the multi-stat abstract) + the deterministic `_opposite` can't handle "mixed" anchors (OB-L1). **VERDICT: the dep(a) 5/5 / dep(b) 4/4 / coarse 4/4 feasibility validations used hand-curated synthetic bundles + curated answers and did NOT generalize to the real pipeline — on real retrievals C1 is unusable in BOTH configs (structural = no precision; primary = no recall). C1 needs FUNDAMENTAL REWORK, not tuning.** Shadow-first caught it before any user exposure (the build's purpose). Caveats: 43 cases, 1 generator, 1 run; FP denom (16) is direction-ADJACENT (C1's mis-fire rate on the answers it acts on), NOT a site-wide rate. → TECH_DEBT (new C1 entry) + BACKLOG §706a (b)/(c).

**C1 follow-up DIAGNOSTIC — conflation is a CHEAP-MODEL artifact, NOT task-intrinsic (2026-06-17, `scripts/direction_extract_probe.py`, `direction_extract_probe_20260617_153856.json`; route now B2B-interim so advisory, not launch-gating; $0.022, 5 cases, no regeneration):** isolated re-run of ONLY the two ③ sub-tasks with a STRONGER model (gpt-4.1, not mini) on captured answers. **(a) extraction:** gpt-4.1 cleanly disentangled the multi-stat conflation trap — P-L1 + P-L2 both got polypharmacy `OR 0.78 (decreases)` AND named the `multimorbidity OR 2.04 (increases)` trap (2/2 — the exact case the mini scorer failed); clean controls 2/2. **(b) LLM comparator** (replacing deterministic `_opposite`): detection 2/2 on conflation reversals, FP 0/2 on clean. **VERDICT: C1's primary-path failure = cheap scorer + brittle comparator, NOT the task → ③ HAS a path as a thin B2B backstop IF built with a strong scorer (gpt-4.1, cents/answer) + LLM comparator.** STILL UNRESOLVED (don't over-read 2/2): the structural over-fire (75% FP) is model-independent and must be DROPPED; anchor-SELECTION over real retrievals untested (probe fed the known anchor); single-source "mixed"/U-shaped abstracts still oversimplified even by gpt-4.1 (OB-L1 dropped the U-shaped caveat). So ③ is not dead, but "viable" is CONDITIONAL on drop-structural + prove-selection-at-scale + handle-mixed.

**RETRIEVAL-RECALL REFUSAL lever — SHADOW DETECTION BUILT + VALIDATED → PASSES (2026-06-18, `retrieval_refusal_20260618_095609.json` + `_prior_probe_095754.json`; plan-back `retrieval_refusal_planback.md`; code committed locally, NOT pushed, Fly deploy deferred, flag default OFF):** covers the failure ③/C1 is STRUCTURALLY BLIND to — a confident answer where the protective/counter source was NEVER retrieved (the obesity-type retrieval-miss; the worst-case that is LIVE now for walk-in users). Route is B2B-interim; this is the advisor's "if the pool is one-directional, refuse to advise without counter-evidence" circuit-breaker + future SaMD defense arch. Gate 1 HEALTHY. **Design = H1 (POOL-STANCE-SPREAD × CONTESTED-PRIOR): refuse = (pool one-sided on factor→outcome) AND (a counter-direction is genuinely contested in CURRENT literature).** Built: `api/services/retrieval_refusal.py` (async, gpt-4.1 — mini conflates), `server.py` shadow hook `_run_retrieval_refusal_background` behind `RETRIEVAL_REFUSAL_SHADOW` (default OFF, mirrors the C1 hook, touches no SSE/answer), `scripts/retrieval_refusal_eval.py` + `_prior_probe.py`. Corpus frozen pre-run (`tests/retrieval_refusal_corpus.json`: detection OB-L0/1/2 + complete-pool P-L1/2 + over-refusal control n=9 incl. counterintuitive-but-SETTLED stress cases; criterion frozen first). Reused captured pools (no re-retrieval). Reads the FINAL pool the generator saw (`documents`). Mid-build refinement: the per-source read is STANCE/valence (harmful/beneficial/mixed), not strict mortality-HR — a harm-framed mechanism review counts as harmful (spot-checked: OB-L2 run2's `beneficial` reads are REAL paradox mentions). **REAL NUMBERS — the OPPOSITE of C1: DETECTION 3/3 retrieval-miss cases fired (by run 7/9; the 2 non-fires correctly had paradox-conveying pools); OVER-REFUSAL 0/9 (with 8/9 one-sided pools = the prior leg genuinely stressed); complete-pool 0/2 over-fire; PRIOR generalization 10/10 contested-vs-settled across diverse topics (glucose-ACCORD/DBP/alcohol/sodium/HRT/obesity True; peanut/BB-HFrEF/smoking/statins False); cost $0.16.** The C1 "flag everything" failure did NOT recur — the prior leg separates genuinely-contested (obesity paradox → refuse) from counterintuitive-but-settled (CAST/LEAP/smoking-PD → don't refuse). H2/H3 NOT needed. **Caveats (do NOT over-read):** end-to-end DETECTION validated on ONE contested topic (obesity, 3 framings — the only retrieval-miss topic captured); prior generalizes 10/10 but other contested topics' live pools firing is untested end-to-end (needs live retrieval); the prior rests on gpt-4.1 world-knowledge (a knowledge dependency — H3 registry could backstop); reused pools, single run, n=9/n=6 modest; per-query firing is pool-composition-dependent (probabilistic, not deterministic); SHADOW DETECTION only (enforcement = A2/route-gated). → BACKLOG (broaden contested-topic detection on live retrieval before enabling). Flag stays OFF.

**LEVER 2 — FRONT-END QUESTION-NEUTRALIZATION — A/B SHADOW BUILT + VALIDATED → SAFE + EFFECTIVE-WHERE-REVERSAL-OCCURS, but NARROW (NOT a cross-topic pass) (2026-06-18, `question_neutralization_20260618_110411.json` + de-risk `_104531.json`; plan-back `question_neutralization_planback.md`; committed locally, NOT pushed, flag default OFF):** the OTHER failure mode (vs Lever 1) — GENERATION-reversal, source-in-hand: protective source retrieved but a LOADED framing makes the generator state the direction backwards. Lever rewrites the INTERNAL generator question to neutral (retrieval + user-facing question untouched). NOT a pure-observer shadow — it changes the answer, so the shadow is an **A/B harness** (both arms on the SAME frozen context, retrieve-once-freeze). Gate 1 HEALTHY. Built: `api/services/question_neutralization.py` (`is_loaded` detector + intent/qualifier-preserving `neutralize`), `server.py` flag-gated rewrite behind `QUESTION_NEUTRALIZATION_SHADOW` (default OFF, fail-open), `scripts/question_neutralization_eval.py`, corpus `tests/question_neutralization_corpus.json` (5 source-in-hand topics, vague-loaded framings + clean control n=8). Design = **detect-then-neutralize** (confirmed: detector 0/16 over-trigger on clean → safe to gate). **REAL NUMBERS — per topic (loaded→neutral REVERSED, /10): polypharmacy 4/10 → 0/10 (ELIMINATED ✅); beta_blockers_hfref 0/10→0/10; beta_carotene 0/10→0/10; cast 0/10→0/10; peanut retrieval-miss.** **HONEST VERDICT: reversal-reduction demonstrated on POLYPHARMACY ONLY** — the other 3 source-in-hand topics do NOT reverse under loaded framing (verified REAL faithful answers, not judge misses: BB "cornerstone… lower mortality HR 0.66"; beta-carotene "RCTs showed harm"). Per the founder's bar, only-polypharmacy ≠ cross-topic pass. The limiting factor is NOT the lever — **source-in-hand loaded-reversal is a NARROW phenomenon largely confined to the conflation-trap structure** (multimorbidity OR 1.82/2.04 co-located with polypharmacy OR 0.78 → vague-loaded framing grabs the harm stat); clean single-direction topics stay faithful. **OVER-NEUTRALIZATION (gating risk) PASSES strongly:** detector over-trigger 0/16, intent/qualifier-retention failures 0/16 (every clinical qualifier preserved), 0 REAL clean-regressions (both 1/8 "regressions" are generation-stochasticity artifacts — CLN-5's neutralized question was IDENTICAL to the original). Cost $1.11 + $0.45. **Net: the lever is SAFE + eliminates reversal where it occurs, but its addressable surface is narrow.** Two-lever coverage = Lever 1 (retrieval-miss, obesity-type, PASS) + Lever 2 (source-in-hand loaded reversal, polypharmacy-type, narrow). Caveats: 4 source-in-hand topics, 2 framings/topic, N=5, single frozen context/topic (reversal is pool-dependent). Flag stays OFF; A2/enforcement route-gated. → BACKLOG.

**DECISION STATE — OPEN (not decided):** founder wants BOTH B2C + B2B viable and agreed to "fix the most serious problems first" rather than pause B2C outright. The route (B2C-after-fix vs B2B-interim) is **PENDING**. **Update (2026-06-15): BOTH "reduce-bad-generation" paths are tried-and-insufficient for B2C** — the prompt-fix (PARTIAL; loaded-framing persists) AND the o4-mini model-swap (0/3 fidelity on B01-loaded). **Update (2026-06-16 #1): the defense roadmap must cover BOTH failure modes** (generation-reversal + retrieval-recall failure); ③ post-hoc checker is **blind** to the retrieval half. **Update (2026-06-16 #2): the retrieval-PROMPT-fix lever is now tried-and-failed (see above) → direction changes.** The retrieval-recall failure (obesity-type) is **hard to fix at the retrieval layer** (deeper levers = lay→MeSH term-expansion or two-sided retrieval = "Path B, deferred"), so it shifts to being **ABSORBED by ① structural-citation fallback** (detect missing protective coverage → low-confidence/withhold/flag, rather than guaranteeing retrieval). **NEW PRIMARY BUILD FOCUS = the GENERATION-reversal half** (polypharmacy-type, source-in-hand): ③ post-hoc direction checker + structural-citation binding (§706a (b)+(c)) — the confidently-actionable half. **Net roadmap: (C) generation-side defenses (③ / structural binding) + (A) ① fallback to absorb retrieval-miss; (B) deeper retrieval-stabilization deferred.** This **strengthens (does not decide) the lean toward "B2B-interim first; B2C gated on defensive mechanisms, not on model choice."** PREREQUISITE for any further eval run: OpenAI auto-recharge ON / balance sufficient (TECH_DEBT P0 ops).

**Framing discipline (keep in all docs):** the QA is EVIDENCE, not an endorsement; the unsupported/contradicts rate is a **lower bound** (abstract-only); the adversarial rate is an **upper bound** (loaded). NLI/contradiction-gate feasibility was assessed (NOT built) — verdict PARTIAL-not-reliable (see TECH_DEBT). OpenAI auto-recharge is OFF + prepaid credit expires → credit-zero returns `[ERROR]` to all users, a launch-gating stability risk (TECH_DEBT).

- **2026-08-12** [fly 231 · HOTFIX B4.1a] **Panel pin/overlap defect — ✅ SHIPPED** — code `4be2f55`; readback exact. **The defect (live on fly 230, founder screenshot):** the cards section scrolled OVER the pinned black panel while the dots were still showing. **Root cause, two layers:** (H2, primary) the pinned section's parent was `flex flex-col` — ScrollTrigger's pin-spacer became a FLEX ITEM and flex sizing squished it, so the `+=350%` reservation never held; (H1-adjacent, secondary) `useFadeIn` permanently retained `transform: translateY(0)` on the cards section — a stacking context that painted the later-DOM cards OVER the `position:fixed` panel once the spacer failure let them meet. **Fix:** wrapper flex removed (children are stacked block sections; the spacer now reserves in normal block flow) + the fade hook clears all inline styles on `transitionend`. **Verified with a real browser scroll-through (dev + Chrome):** pin-spacer 3823px, `position:fixed` held across the range, width scrubs 70→95vw, segments advance, reverse clean, **zero overlap at 13 measured scroll positions**; static path verified structurally (it is the SSR default; untouched by the fix — Gate 5 rows 4–5 stay the founder's check). **LESSON: entry-animation transforms and ScrollTrigger pins don't mix — exclude pinned sections from generic entry animations, clear transform residue on completion, and never give a pinned section a flex parent.**

  > **📌 COUNSEL RULING 1 — THE DATE MUST TRACK CONTENT.** *The "Last updated" date must be updated whenever content materially changes, because it establishes **which contract version binds a user at a given time**.* Both pages were stale: `/terms` said **2026-05-08** but gained §11 at fly 222; `/privacy` said **2026-06-12** but changed at fly 222, 223 **and** 224.   > **📌 COUNSEL RULING 2 — NO NOTIFICATION DUTY HERE, AND THIS IS THE REUSABLE PART.** These changes are **「更有利或中性的補充」** — beneficial or neutral additions — so **no notice is owed**. 🔴 **THE TEST FOR WHEN A DUTY *WOULD* ARISE — a MATERIALLY ADVERSE change. Counsel's three examples: a PRICE RISE · NEW THIRD-PARTY DATA SHARING · NARROWED USER RIGHTS.** **Apply this test before assuming any future legal-copy edit is notice-free.** **Nothing was built** — no banner, no modal, no email. Counsel offered an optional non-blocking banner as best practice; **the founder did not authorise it, so it does not exist.**
  > **🔴 THE ARCHIVE'S RECONSTRUCTION IS ITSELF THE FINDING: a "Last updated" LABEL NEVER UNIQUELY IDENTIFIED A TEXT.** **`Last updated: 2026-06-12` labelled FOUR different Privacy Policy texts** (v07–v10); **`2026-05-08` labelled two different Terms texts** (v06–v07); and **both pages changed on 2026-04-01 while still displaying "March 2026"**. So the drift corrected today was not a one-off oversight — **the date has been decoupled from the content since March.** The archive is therefore keyed on the **distinct text**, with the displayed label recorded alongside so the two reconcile.
  > **`docs/legal-versions/` — 17 snapshots (7 Terms · 10 Privacy) + a README index** giving each version's effective range, introducing commit, displayed label, and what changed. Prose extracted **verbatim** from the JSX at each commit; the extractor **asserts** no unconsumed markup or JSX brace survives and **fails rather than emitting a partial record**. Footer nav links excluded as chrome, and that exclusion is stated.

- **2026-08-11** [fly 225] **`headerTagline` converged · guard exclusions now ZERO · PHASE E filed — ✅ SHIPPED** — code `f5fca68` + `9456372`. **fly v225, `.\deploy.ps1` clean.** ✅ **Readback:** `/health` `revision` = `945637245c40…` = pushed SHA, exact match.

  > 🔴 **HONESTY FINDING — A FALSE PROVENANCE CLAIM WAS LIVE ON THE BENGALI PUBLIC PAGES, AND THIS SHIP REMOVED IT.** `api/i18n/explore_strings.py` rendered `headerTagline` as **「সরকারি সূত্র দ্বারা যাচাইকৃত」 — *"verified by GOVERNMENT sources"***. **`সরকারি` denotes the state**, not "official" in the generic sense (cf. `সরকারি হাসপাতাল` = *government hospital*); the neutral word is `অফিসিয়াল`, which `utils/i18n-share.ts` used. **The English claims neither government verification nor state endorsement, and Vela cannot support either on a medical product.** It shipped on the Bengali `/explore` and blog pages — the surfaces strangers reach from search. **Fixed by converging to the `অফিসিয়াল` wording. Recorded as its own finding, not folded into the merge, because it was a live defect independent of the convergence.** ⚠️ **It was found only because a per-locale claim-strength comparison was required before merging** — a merge that had picked a winner by file or by length would have shipped whichever wording won, with no one reading either.

  > **How it got there, and why the class is not closed:** the same root cause as fly 224 — two files independently machine-translated one English source and nothing compared them. **MT does not preserve claim strength**, and no test in this repo could ever have caught it: the strings were *valid*, *present*, and *well-formed*. **PHASE E is filed for this reason** (see below); the guard makes divergence visible, it does not make a translation honest.
  > **STEP 1 — per-locale characterisation before any edit.** `headerTagline` = *"Ask in your language. Verified by official sources."* — a provenance claim on every public page in 16 languages. **6 of 16 already agreed** (en · zh-TW · it · pt · hi · vi — verified identical *before* being left alone). **10 diverged**, in three distinct kinds:
  > **(a) claim ESCALATED — bn** (above). **(b) claim GRAMMAR changed — ar**: `i18n-share.ts` lacks the passive marker and reads as an **imperative** (*"check the official sources"*), shifting verification onto the reader; explore's `تم التحقق` is the completed passive matching the English. **Arabic explore wins.** *(The `تم` construction is mechanically identifiable; whether a native reader parses the other form as imperative in context is **UNDETERMINED**.)* **(c) CONTENT DROPPED — fr**: explore reads *"Posez votre question."*, **dropping `dans votre langue` entirely** — the first clause loses its whole meaning — and writes `par sources officielles` without the article.
  > **The remaining 7 are equal in claim strength**, so the fly-224 default applied: **de** `verifiziert` vs `geprüft` (checked — slightly more general, not weaker in kind), **ja** `公式ソース` vs `公式情報源` (naturalness, **UNDETERMINED**), **ko** full sentences vs clipped noun forms, **es** register only — `Pregunte` (usted) vs `Pregunta` (tú); **usted kept as the professional register and flagged for the reviewer rather than decided here**, **th** `แหล่งข้อมูลทางการ` vs `แหล่งทางการ`, **zh-CN** punctuation only, **he** `על ידי` (*by*) vs `מ` (*from*).
  > **Per-locale winners, stated not mixed:** `i18n-share.ts` → **ja ko es fr de th bn he**; `explore_strings.py` → **zh-CN** (already-correct fullwidth) and **ar** (correct passive); **untouched** → en zh-TW it pt hi vi. **`api/services/share_renderer.py` needed NO change** — it carries en + zh-TW only and both already agreed. Values copied programmatically, never retyped.

- **2026-08-10** [fly 218] **`/api/share/create` citation contract — typed + bounded, `local` TOMBSTONED AT WRITE — ✅ SHIPPED** — code `d85e85e`, docs `<this commit>`. **Deployed 2026-08-10, fly version 218, nrt, image `deployment-01KZN4R4CZAS7AR8841YQ4Y3XH`; `.\deploy.ps1` clean; machines 683d447c2e5428 started + 2879720c66d478 stopped — both v218, the known benign auto-start false-negative.**
  > **✅ STEP 0 — BUILD CONFIRMED BY READBACK** (the fly-217 capability, used for the first time on a real ship): `/health` `revision` = **`d85e85e388528e06ca6fbfa7cb6c63d6033ce9da`** = the pushed SHA, exact match.
  > **What:** `ShareCreateRequest.citations` was a bare `list` — no item schema, no bounds — stored verbatim onto a **public, SEO-indexed** page. Now `list[CitationIn]`, **max 50**, every string bounded (`url` ≤2048, `snippet`/`abstract`/`text` ≤8000, `source_type` ≤64). **Field set DERIVED, not invented:** the 10 fields of `schemas.Citation` (what the SSE emits and `ShareModal` forwards back) **+ `abstract`/`text`**, which the renderer reads as snippet fallbacks; `id`/`source_id` Optional because stored `explore_page` rows are observed without them.
  > **POSTURE — LENIENT, deliberately NOT reject-on-unknown.** `source_type` and `credibility` are bounded **strings, not enums**: rejecting unknowns would break the open **#7** population (stale clients replaying pre-c1 answers carrying `local`) and would 422 on **`clinical-trial`/`review`**, valid `CredibilityLevel` members the renderer has no pill for. **The hole closed is the UNBOUNDED payload, not unknown values.** Only **structural** violations are rejected → normal 422.
  > **Rule 19 — ONE transform, two callers.** `share_renderer.tombstone_citation` is now the single definition, called by `_augment_citations` (render) **and** the store site (write). Data-level only — it bakes in **no render chrome**, because the label is locale-dependent ("Source withdrawn" / 「來源已撤回」) and persisting one would freeze a locale into the row. **Both layers stay:** write side stops any NEW untombstoned row; **render side still covers the 8 pre-c1 stubs deliberately retained in prod** (deleting them renumbers the prose). Removing either reopens a population.
  > **🔴 A COLLISION FOUND AND FIXED IN THE SAME CHANGE:** 422 now has **two senders**. The PHI block sends `type='share_sensitive_blocked'`; FastAPI validation sends `detail` and no `type`. Undiscriminated, a malformed payload would have rendered the **"sensitive content"** copy — telling the user something **false about their own data**. `ShareModal` now discriminates and falls back to the generic error.
  > **Gates: pytest 173 passed** (was 161 — **6 new write-path cases**: local stored tombstoned · pubmed/tfda/dailymed byte-identical · unknown `source_type` accepted untouched · 5 structural payloads rejected · **write-vs-render parity pin** · a guard that the model declares every field the renderer reads, since Pydantic would otherwise drop it silently). **`npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0.** **NO human-eye render gate required and none run** — rendering logic is unchanged and the parity test pins it; the change is write-path plus a one-function refactor.
  > ❌ ~~⏳ **FOUNDER UI SPOT-CHECK REQUESTED**…~~ → ✅ **FOUNDER UI SPOT-CHECK: PASS (2026-08-10, `/q/80DFqlk4jGQ` on fly 218).** **5 citations — 3 DailyMed + 2 PubMed** — all rendering **source labels**, **credibility pills** (Official / Peer Reviewed) and **working View-source deep links**. **No collateral tombstoning of real sources. No FDA chip.** **This closes the fly-218 verification loop:** test (b) held at the data layer, and the founder confirmed it end to end.

- **2026-06-02** [stage3] **Stage 3.1 — next-themes infrastructure + FOUC fix** — **DEPLOYED to prod 2026-06-02 08:43 UTC (fly v171)**. 2 commits (`2b7120c` infra + `c5987bc` FOUC follow-up). Installed `next-themes@^0.4.6` and wrapped the app tree in `<ThemeProvider attribute="class" defaultTheme="dark" enableSystem disableTransitionOnChange>` (inside ClerkProvider, outside LangProvider); removed the hardcoded `className="dark"` from `pages/_document.tsx` so next-themes owns the `<html>` class. FOUC fix: an inline theme-init `<script>` injected into `_document` `<Head>` (next-themes' own script lands at the top of `<body>` in Pages-Router static export — too late, causing a dark flash on cold load); the head script reads `localStorage['theme']`, falls back to `defaultTheme='dark'`, resolves `system` via `matchMedia`, and applies the class before first paint — exactly mirroring next-themes' runtime resolution (no class toggle on hydration → no flash, no mismatch). **`defaultTheme="dark"` preserves v170's visual exactly**: functional pages still render dark, landing still renders light via its inner `.light` wrapper. Andrew verified on prod: cold load + incognito + slow-3G show no flash; `<html class="dark">`, `theme=dark` in localStorage, no console hydration warning, OS-toggle is a no-op (default overrides system until 3.8). Migration: NONE. **Still cosmetic until 3.2**: the Step 5 `SettingsControls` + `LandingSettingsDropdown` theme toggles remain `useState`-only — clicking a theme card does not change page color yet.
